# Minds 9: spatial hoarding campaign outcomes

The complete declared campaign ran on 2026-10-02: **81 conditions × 40 comparison seeds and 21 cells × 5 separate timing seeds**, with all 3,345 full envelopes saved before analysis. Every one-season cell returned its episode; every multi-generation cell returned all 60 seasons. No run terminated as extinct or zero-fitness. The principal outcome is conditional: an unguarded all-larder strategy loses survival against scatter with watching off or at span 2, but gains at span 7. Paid guarding reduces survival in every matched seed at all three watching settings, despite preventing theft and increasing owner recovery. These are measured outcomes under the approved protocol, with no Holds/Fails or stability verdicts.

## Main findings and interpretation

The primary fixed-strategy comparison is **L=1 minus L=0, guard off**, using one 200-tick season. It changes concentration, real home delivery, travel and theft opportunities together. Its name in the manifest, `home-travel`, does not make it an isolated travel-cost estimate. Watching off, span 2 and span 7 give paired mean survival differences of **−10.24, −4.50 and +18.64 percentage points**, respectively. The descriptive 95% intervals are [−11.69, −8.80], [−5.86, −3.14] and [+17.28, +20.00] points; signs are 0/0/40, 6/1/33 and 40/0/0 positive/zero/negative. The associated scatter versus larder survival means are 85.51% versus 75.27%, 86.67% versus 82.17%, and 63.87% versus 82.51%. The L=0.5 cells remain descriptive: guard-off means are 89.40%, 90.57% and 88.74%; there is no fitted optimum or promoted intermediate-strategy claim.

**Guard on minus off at L=1** reduces survival by **30.50, 45.26 and 46.69 points**, with negative signs in all 40 seeds in every stratum. Intervals are [−31.85, −29.15], [−46.91, −43.60] and [−48.02, −45.36] points. At span 7, guarding raises the mean per-seed larder recovery fraction from 1.75% to 32.27% and reduces mean pilfered larder food from 41,764.66 to 13,727.56 units. But it consumes 5,750.73 paid turns, delivered food falls from 45,048.82 to 20,377.17, and closing holdings fall from 2,611.55 to 471.82. Guarding changes later stocks, exposure, deliveries and survival together; these measurements demonstrate its cost here without decomposing all causal pathways. It provides perfect blocking while active, an upper-bound protection assumption, yet is costly enough to reduce survival.

Food continues to circulate through theft. Owner recovery of scatter in the L=0 fixed cells is only 0.30–0.59% of buried food, while mean scatter food pilfered is 65,989.83–72,034.12 units. Both consumption and demand, burial and owner-death losses, holdings and exposed/closing stocks are reported separately below. Nearly fulfilled aggregate metabolic demand does not mean all founders survived: demand stops after death. Exposure rates use pre-step cache/food-unit ticks, rather than observed theft candidates or final closing stock.

**Discovery evolution does not give a calibrated apparency threshold.** With ordinary survival selection, scatter find=1 versus 0.25 raises endpoint mean L by 0.03835, 0.08700 and 0.12756 at off/2/7; signs are 31/0/9, 38/0/2 and 40/0/0. The lower find settings are not monotonic across strata: at span 7, find=0, 0.02 and 0.05 all lower endpoint L relative to 0.25 (−0.04975, −0.05115, −0.03667), while the watching-off differences are small and mostly have intervals crossing zero. All 12 declared discovery contrasts, all five cells per stratum and all trajectories are retained. Contact-conditioned spatial `find` is not Vander Wall and Jenkins's apparency parameter, so neither a 0.219 match nor a fitted breakpoint is claimed.

**Selection must be read against inheritance drift.** Survival-selected endpoint mean L is 0.16864/0.18607/0.18524 at off/2/7, versus the same Neutral endpoint 0.20681 in each stratum. The paired Survival-minus-Neutral L differences are −0.03817/−0.02074/−0.02157; their 95% intervals exclude zero. Survival differences against Neutral are small, and their intervals include zero in all three strata. Endpoint D differences against Neutral also have intervals crossing zero. The realized initial means are L=0.17272 and D=0.50173, not their sampler centers. With h²=1 and Vseg=0, the Neutral endpoint is L=0.15080/D=0.50272; default Neutral is L=0.20681/D=0.53014. Regression/noise and logit transformation therefore visibly change means even without mortality selection. Continuous interior values alone establish neither a stable mixture nor a categorical-strategy equilibrium.

Stores selection, based on living closing cache food and excluding holdings, gives endpoint L=0.04167/0.09409/0.09938 and D=0.59453/0.61113/0.59507. Stores-minus-Survival L differences are −0.12697/−0.09198/−0.08586, while D differences are +0.06565/+0.09023/+0.07006. Its survival differences are −0.00729/+0.00386/−0.01329, rather than a uniform survival gain. **D raises the defense target; it does not increase protection strength.** Larger D reduces guard probability at a given larder stock. These are distinct selection objectives, not alternative definitions chosen after seeing results. All 63 sensitivity contrasts and founder/parent-weight distributions are retained, including slope, stumble-order, inheritance and Neutral+inheritance controls.

**Frozen strategy contests show frequency-dependent directions without a stability verdict.** L=0/D=0.5 remains exact, and flags follow the first parent jointly. Initial rare/middle/common shares are 17/175, 87/175 and 157/175. With watching off, rare forgoing cheaters increase in 34/40 seeds (mean change +0.73529); middle and common starts become entirely cheaters in every seed. With span 2, the enabled joint cheating/watching strategy disappears from all rare and middle runs but reaches share 1 in every common run. Thus rare and common directions oppose at span 2, with associated survival 85.51% versus 47.74%; they do not show convergence to an interior mix. With span 7, rare scroungers disappear in all seeds and middle starts decline in all seeds; common starts decrease on average by 0.51086 but increase in 15 seeds and decrease in 25, with endpoint mean share 0.38629 and survival 71.17%. This is heterogeneous across run seeds, not a single stable mixture. The forgo rule retains fallback harvesting when no fresh remembered target applies; these are not pure parasites.

Among **burying watchers**, span 2 rare and common starts change by +0.17857 and +0.02671, with positive/zero/negative signs 25/0/15 and 29/1/10; the common interval crosses zero. At span 7, rare starts rise by +0.23157 (31/0/9), while common starts fall by −0.12400 (16/0/24). The intervals for both span-7 means exclude zero, and endpoint whole-cohort survival is 88.16% for the rare start and 75.77% for the common start. These frozen-trait rare/common directions support a limited frequency-dependence discussion, but do not establish attraction, stationarity or a stable-mixture classification. Middle starts and all individual seed trajectories remain visible. Mechanism-off watcher flags still drift under inheritance but watching/spread contrasts are explicitly unused. Enabled scrounger contrasts jointly change cheating and watching, so they cannot isolate either alone. Endpoint group-survival gaps are unavailable whenever a group disappears; those seeds are listed, never removed from an average.

The **guard-harvest probes** remain separate sensitivities of the continuous baseline. They allow site harvest only after a paid guard turn with no own recovery. Mean endpoint probe food is 906.05/515.39/505.33 units at off/2/7, while survival differences against ordinary guarding are −0.00171/+0.00443/+0.00314, all with intervals crossing zero. They do not replace the primary fixed-strategy guard comparisons or show how much of their survival cost is due to foregone harvest.

The serial timing baselines average **11.753/14.440/18.108 microseconds per live-at-tick-start agent-tick** and **0.390684/0.484348/0.605819 seconds per returned generation** at off/2/7 (five timing seeds per cell). The full tables retain all 105 raw times, biological results and actual denominators, including the slower fixed scatter-watching cells. These wall-clock observations apply to this machine/build; no hardware-independent speed claim or unrelated-engine comparison is made.

## Mechanisms and research boundary

Each world has 175 founders, one good, capacity 50, reserve horizon 20, a 100-tick summer followed by a 100-tick winter, winter divisor 32, half-surplus allocation, even caching, owner memory and walk speed 1. Ordinary births, replacement and incompatible mechanisms are disabled. **L is the probability that a positive batch allocation goes to the larder**, not a fraction of every food batch; exact 0/1 endpoints take the deterministic branch. A larder allocation remains carried food, consuming capacity and exposed to metabolism until delivered. Real home contact is the home cell or a non-wall orthogonal neighbor; merely occupying a home grants no immunity. Larders have a larger contact area than a scatter cache.

For reserve R, the defense target is T=max(R,1)+D×capacity; guard probability is logistic(slope×(larder/T−0.5)), ordinary slope 10. Eligible noncheating owners at tick-start home contact with positive larder stock draw intentions in agent-id order before the normal shuffle. Executed guards hold position and forgo harvest and foreign theft; own recovery remains possible. All foreign larder takes are blocked while the living owner guards, and owner death removes protection immediately. Slope 5/20 changes the intention curve, not blocking strength.

Arrival precedence is own scatter recovery, own larder recovery, observed larder raid, observed scatter raid, larder stumble, scatter stumble, then harvest. `scatter-first` is an explicit sensitivity. Watch memory records a witnessed deposition/home and deposited amount, not a permanently known omniscient inventory; stale, empty and denied memories are forgotten. Ordinary raids use `raid_if=better`. Off/2/7 are separate strata, not evidence of a universal memory-span effect. Detailed observations, delivery events, guard counters and kind flow fields remain in full raw envelopes.

Every season constructs a fresh world with repeated episode seed, maps, bodies and endowments. Cache food, holdings, memories, paths, delivery intentions and guards reset; no material or environmental inheritance exists. Boundary reset is not charged to metabolism, pilferage or owner-death loss. Only the specified traits/flags inherit. Survival gives alive founders weight 1 and dead founders 0. Neutral gives all archived founders, including dead ones, weight 1 unless the cohort is completely extinct; it is an explicit counterfactual. Stores gives living founders their closing scatter+larder food, excluding holdings; there is no subsidy or uniform fallback. Complete extinction and live zero Stores fitness terminate distinctly.

Continuous inheritance is child logit=h²×midparent+(1−h²)×dead-inclusive population mean+Normal(0,Vseg), with finite clamps, default h²=0.8/Vseg=0.5. The initial independent logit-normal trait centers are 0.15/0.5 with variance 0.5. Initial sampling, repeated episodes and continuing breeding use separate PCG64Mcg streams seeded with the run seed; this separation is not a claim that paired biological outcomes are statistically independent. Frozen contests copy continuous traits exactly, without regression/noise, and copy both flags from the first parent. All founders, dead or alive, remain in archived trait means and endpoint distributions. Generation 59 denotes the cohort used in season 60 after 59 breedings, not a newly bred post-season endpoint.

Run seed is the sampling unit, ordinarily n=40. All 129 declarations use matched A−B differences (or explicit zero for within-run share change), positive/zero/negative counts and descriptive two-sided 95% Student t intervals, df=39. They are unadjusted for multiplicity. Duplicated settings in separate panels, generations and founder slots are not additional samples. Every returned trajectory and terminal endpoint is retained, with no extension to an unobserved generation. Undefined ratios are absent/null, not zero; a single missing declared seed makes the entire paired summary unavailable, with the defined values and missing identities retained. The unused three watcher-off share-change summaries are intentionally absent, not missing raw runs.

The detailed FIFO fate ledger is **not_collected** in this route. Aggregate conservation, stocks and always-on exposures are authoritative; no fate-age, fate-fraction or complete-ledger claim follows. No parameter, threshold, contrast, seed or primary designation changed in response to these outcomes. The results neither reproduce a calibrated biological threshold nor imply safety, regulation, shared minds, communication or collective-agency features. The next campaign remains **P3 protection/P4 deception**: less absolute defense, watched-cache concealment/recaching and misleading observers need new approved designs. Affordances, learning, persistent communication and collective agency remain later proposed campaigns in the [program design](../../docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-02-minds-collective-agency-program-design.md).

## Provenance, completeness and reproduction

The [approved design](../../docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-02-minds-9-spatial-hoarding-design.md) and [judging amendment](../../docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-02-minds-9-judge-amendment.md) bind this campaign. User approval (“Yeah, let's continue!”) is recorded in committed amendment revision **db0d8338f563c5d84d07996897b0355d67cf6aef**. Execution code is **a23d29ae8fea905bb04b52b7781c772de2507828**, a clean working tree before the run. The manifest has 81 comparison cells, 21 timing cells and 129 declared contrasts. Amendment SHA256 is `d071c674271c10207d1daf3b62398d3e8cda9afad3298c1826edfde0303cb0ae`; manifest-output SHA256 is `412e45644b2bc8cd837adf7a7f1388cbbd22d64987350fa94fe24f39a9d34bb5`.

Preflight `cargo test --manifest-path survey/Cargo.toml minds9` passed 36 deterministic tests (81 filtered out), before the full command. `--minds9 --help` and `--minds9 --manifest` established syntax and exact cells. The actual full command, from the repository root, was:

```sh
cargo run --release --manifest-path survey/Cargo.toml -- --minds9 --run --amendment-revision db0d8338f563c5d84d07996897b0355d67cf6aef --out survey/out/results-minds9-a23d29a.json
```

It exited 0. Eight comparison workers used default `min(available_parallelism,8)`; one coordinator flushed/synced full envelopes before canonical index entries, with bounded completed-envelope memory. All workers joined before the serial timing/replay phase. Toolchain: rustc 1.98.1 (`48a229cea`, 2026-09-01), cargo 1.98.1 (`797e8a9bc`, 2026-08-05), Node v24.6.0, npm 11.5.1. Hardware: Apple M1 Max, arm64, 10 logical CPUs, 64 GiB RAM, macOS 15.6.1. The release run began at 18:16 UTC; raw output was covered by the existing ignored `survey/out/results-*.json` pattern with 329 GiB free before launch.

An earlier sequential execution at db0d8338 was stopped solely for the reviewed scheduling optimization, not because of scientific failure or unfavorable outcomes. Its 253 completed envelopes and 1,020 generation records remain at `survey/out/results-minds9-db0d8338.json/`; no in-flight partial file was indexed or left behind. Every overlapping complete envelope was compared exactly against the new campaign, excluding only execution code/pool provenance: **253 matched, 0 missing, 0 biological differences**, with no numeric tolerance. These preserved superseded records are not pooled into the final results.

The final canonical index is `survey/out/results-minds9-a23d29a.json/index.json`, SHA256 `8a2b55c07e83257c0bf30e77e1c149adb254d95061c508f64c253ac40ca1927c`. Its role/id/seed paths cover every declared record exactly once: **3,240 comparisons / 151,920 generation rows**, **105 timings / 990 generation rows**, **9,083,799,415 raw-envelope bytes**. Each fixed cell has 40 one-generation runs; every multi-generation cell has 40 60-generation runs; every timing cell has all five seeds. All 3,345 terminal statuses are null. Saved initial cohorts match first archived cohorts; seed roles, cohort/flag initialization, generation numbering, exact parent weights, frozen traits, lineage and reset-dependent conservation were checked. All 105 timing biological envelopes exactly match their comparison-role counterparts; timing ticks, live-at-tick-start agent-ticks, returned generations and raw seconds were checked. Maximum kind conservation residuals are 1.983e−10 scatter and 7.049e−11 larder food units. Empty-kind flow/exposure zeros remain real zeros; their ratios remain absent.

The production analyzer revalidates the full configuration/cohort/draw-order/weight/identity contract. Independently streaming every indexed envelope checked kind conservation, capacities, dead stocks, exposures, metabolic demand/consumption, guard eligibility counters, delivery consistency, ordinary-probe zeros, parent weights and timing matches. This is complemented by the deterministic implementation tests of reset and arrival semantics; aggregate audit alone is not a direct observation of every transient world field.

To reproduce analysis without running a world, use the saved index and a fresh ignored destination:

```sh
cargo run --release --manifest-path survey/Cargo.toml -- --minds9 --analyze survey/out/results-minds9-a23d29a.json/index.json --out survey/out/results-minds9-a23d29a-reanalysis.json
```

This also exited 0 and reproduced `analysis.json` (429,877,145 bytes, SHA256 `d529a25faf64ad322f3843cb564d6e1fb82591ae61a17773bcf851d90b4fe3fe`) and native `results.md` (40,528 bytes, SHA256 `8c29477a5751b8832c52fdc3d83bf0d79224f97f44fced3c6dbd6ba774d6a892`) **byte for byte**. The raw directory contains `runs/`, index, machine analysis and native rendered report. Raw outputs remain ignored local research artifacts; this tracked report includes every declared summary and exact seedwise difference, expanded systematic tables, and the supplement-generation script below. Full archived founders, lineage and all 60 trajectory/distribution rows per evolved run remain reproducible from the retained raw index. Report numbers are rounded for reading, while seedwise differences use 17 significant digits.


## Native measured tables

Code `a23d29ae8fea905bb04b52b7781c772de2507828`; amendment `db0d8338f563c5d84d07996897b0355d67cf6aef`; raw index `survey/out/results-minds9-a23d29a.json/index.json`. Protocol: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-02-minds-9-judge-amendment.md`.

Comparison execution used 8 workers. Timing runs and tick replays ran serially after all comparison workers joined.

No Holds/Fails verdicts. Run seed is the sample unit. Endpoints use the last observed archived cohort, including dead founders; terminal endpoints are never extended to generation 59. All trajectories and parent-weight distributions are in analysis.json; complete founders, lineage, weights, initial sampling and draw order are in raw files.

Means use all declared seeds. Completed generations vary after termination. Strategy-contest frequency changes are frozen-trait measures; interior shares alone do not establish stability. Mechanism-off watcher flags remain inherited, but watching advantage is unused. Scrounger enabled contests jointly change cheating and watching.

Return turns and paid guard turns measure displaced opportunities. Larder-minus-scatter combines concentration with delivery and travel; these panels do not isolate a pure travel effect. Guard-on/off changes paid defense; blocked attempts alone do not measure the survival benefit. Ordinary guard probe food is an actual zero. The active guard-harvest sensitivity is reported separately below.

### Selection and founder endpoint distributions

Each row gives per-run endpoint distribution statistics averaged across all 40 seeds, including dead founders. Parent weights use exact archived selection weights: Survival=alive, Neutral=1 for every archived founder unless terminal, Stores=living closing scatter+larder. All terminal parent weights are zero. Exact founder values, lineage and each distribution at every returned generation remain in the raw envelopes and `analysis.json`. Endpoint rows keep each seed’s actual observed generation.

### Evolution trajectories

All returned generation trajectories, including every terminal row and every founder/weight distribution, remain in the raw records and machine analysis. The systematic checkpoints below cover every declared multi-generation cell at archived generations 0, 9, 19, 39 and 59. Generation 59 is the founder cohort used in the 60th seasonal episode, not a newly bred cohort after that season. A checkpoint is unavailable if any of its 40 seeds terminated earlier; no terminal value is carried forward and no reduced-seed mean is presented. The observed endpoint table above retains every run even when a late checkpoint is absent.

### Frozen contest group survival and rare/common starts

The gap is flagged survival minus unflagged survival, each using its own archived founder denominator. Generation 0 follows the supplied rare/middle/common starting cohort. Endpoint gaps become undefined if either strategy group disappears; no reduced-seed average substitutes for a complete contrast. Enabled scrounger flags jointly encode cheating and watching; mechanism-off watcher flags remain inherited while their watching mechanism and spread interpretation are unused.

### Guard-harvest probes

These are separate sensitivity cells. The probe allows current-cell harvest only after a paid guard turn whose own recovery took no food. It is not ordinary guarding and is not substituted into the primary guard contrasts. Values below are actual endpoint episode per-run means; full probe trajectories and baseline contrasts remain in the declared table and machine analysis.

## Reproduce the supplementary tables

```sh
python3 reproduce-minds9-supplement.py survey/out/results-minds9-a23d29a.json > minds9-supplement.md
```

Save the following script as a local Python file and invoke it with the complete raw-output directory as its sole argument. It reads `analysis.json` and writes only Markdown to stdout; it changes no raw record or analysis setting. The native tables above are generated by the reviewed survey CLI.

<details>
<summary>Supplement generator (Python standard library)</summary>

```python
"""Read-only reproducible report supplement from a complete CLI machine analysis."""
import collections,json,pathlib,statistics,sys
p=pathlib.Path(sys.argv[1]);a=json.loads((p/'analysis.json').read_text());measured=a['measured'];conditions=measured['manifest']['conditions'];runs=measured['runs'];by=collections.defaultdict(list)
assert len(runs)==3240 and len(a['timings'])==105
for r in runs:by[r['condition_id']].append(r)
assert all(sorted(r['seed'] for r in by[c['id']])==list(range(1,41)) for c in conditions)
out=[]
def emit(s=''):out.append(s)
def fmt(x):return f'{x:.6f}'
def mean_metric(rows,key,initial=False):
    vals=[(r['seed'],r['trajectory'][0]['metrics'][key] if initial else r['endpoint']['metrics'][key]) for r in rows]
    missing=[s for s,x in vals if x is None]
    if missing:return 'unavailable; missing '+','.join(map(str,missing))
    return fmt(statistics.mean(x for s,x in vals))
emit('## Additional flows, stocks, opportunities and distributions')
emit();emit('Every cell below uses all 40 run seeds. Amounts are food units. Ratios are arithmetic means of the per-seed ratios, available only when all declared seeds define the denominator; missing seeds remain explicit. Cache exposure is pre-step positive-cache ticks, with final closing stock excluded. This supplement adds no formal verdict or undeclared threshold.');emit()
emit('| Fixed condition | Kind | Buried | Dug | Pilfered | Owner-death loss | Closing stock | Cache-ticks | Recovery | Pilfers/cache-tick | Loss/food-unit-tick |')
emit('|---|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---|---|---|')
for c in conditions:
    if c['panel']!='fixed':continue
    rows=by[c['id']]
    for kind in ['scatter','larder']:
        keys=[kind+'_'+x for x in ['buried','dug','pilfered','lost']]+['closing_'+kind,kind+'_cache_ticks',kind+'_recovery',kind+'_pilferage_rate',kind+'_loss_rate']
        emit('| '+c['id']+' | '+kind+' | '+' | '.join(mean_metric(rows,k) for k in keys)+' |')
emit();emit('### Fixed delivery, guarding and discovery opportunities');emit()
emit('| Fixed condition | Closing holdings | Delivery starts / completions / cancellations | Guard intentions / executions | Guard recovery | Discovery hits | Guard probe food |')
emit('|---|---:|---|---|---:|---:|---:|')
for c in conditions:
    if c['panel']!='fixed':continue
    rows=by[c['id']];m=lambda k:mean_metric(rows,k)
    emit('| '+c['id']+' | '+m('closing_holdings')+' | '+' / '.join(m('delivery_'+s) for s in ['starts','completions','cancellations'])+' | '+m('guard_intended')+' / '+m('guard_executed')+' | '+m('guard_recovered')+' | '+m('observation_discovery_hits')+' | '+m('guard_probe_harvest')+' |')
emit();emit('Return turns and paid guard turns measure displaced opportunities. Larder-minus-scatter combines concentration with delivery and travel; these panels do not isolate a pure travel effect. Guard-on/off changes paid defense; blocked attempts alone do not measure the survival benefit. Ordinary guard probe food is an actual zero. The active guard-harvest sensitivity is reported separately below.');emit()
emit('### Selection and founder endpoint distributions');emit()
emit('Each row gives per-run endpoint distribution statistics averaged across all 40 seeds, including dead founders. Parent weights use exact archived selection weights: Survival=alive, Neutral=1 for every archived founder unless terminal, Stores=living closing scatter+larder. All terminal parent weights are zero. Exact founder values, lineage and each distribution at every returned generation remain in the raw envelopes and `analysis.json`. Endpoint rows keep each seed’s actual observed generation.');emit()
emit('| Condition | Completed generations min–max | Endpoint weights zero / positive / sum | Endpoint weight min / median / max | Endpoint L min / median / max | Endpoint D min / median / max |')
emit('|---|---|---|---|---|---|')
for c in conditions:
    rows=by[c['id']]
    def d(field,keys):return ' / '.join(fmt(statistics.mean(r['endpoint'][field][k] for r in rows)) for k in keys)
    emit('| '+c['id']+' | '+str(min(r['completed_generations'] for r in rows))+'–'+str(max(r['completed_generations'] for r in rows))+' | '+d('parent_weights',['zero','positive','sum'])+' | '+d('parent_weights',['min','median','max'])+' | '+d('larder_traits',['min','median','max'])+' | '+d('defense_traits',['min','median','max'])+' |')
emit();emit('### Evolution trajectories');emit()
emit('All returned generation trajectories, including every terminal row and every founder/weight distribution, remain in the raw records and machine analysis. The systematic checkpoints below cover every declared multi-generation cell at archived generations 0, 9, 19, 39 and 59. Generation 59 is the founder cohort used in the 60th seasonal episode, not a newly bred cohort after that season. A checkpoint is unavailable if any of its 40 seeds terminated earlier; no terminal value is carried forward and no reduced-seed mean is presented. The observed endpoint table above retains every run even when a late checkpoint is absent.');emit()
emit('<details>');emit('<summary>Every multi-generation cell: systematic trajectory checkpoints</summary>');emit()
emit('| Condition | Archived generation | Returned seed rows | L | D | Survival | Cheater share | Watcher flags (off flags unused for mechanism) |')
emit('|---|---:|---:|---|---|---|---|---|')
for c in conditions:
    if c['panel']=='fixed':continue
    rows=by[c['id']]
    for generation in [0,9,19,39,59]:
        available=[r['trajectory'][generation] for r in rows if len(r['trajectory'])>generation]
        values=['unavailable']*5 if len(available)!=40 else [fmt(statistics.mean(g['metrics'][key] for g in available)) for key in ['mean_larder','mean_defense','survival','cheater_share','watcher_share']]
        emit('| '+c['id']+' | '+str(generation)+' | '+str(len(available))+'/40 | '+' | '.join(values)+' |')
emit();emit('</details>');emit()
emit();emit('### Frozen contest group survival and rare/common starts');emit()
emit('The gap is flagged survival minus unflagged survival, each using its own archived founder denominator. Generation 0 follows the supplied rare/middle/common starting cohort. Endpoint gaps become undefined if either strategy group disappears; no reduced-seed average substitutes for a complete contrast. Enabled scrounger flags jointly encode cheating and watching; mechanism-off watcher flags remain inherited while their watching mechanism and spread interpretation are unused.');emit()
emit('| Contest condition | Generation-0 flagged-minus-unflagged survival | Actual endpoint gap (all seeds or unavailable) |')
emit('|---|---|---|')
for c in conditions:
    if c['panel']!='contests':continue
    rows=by[c['id']];key='cheater_survival_gap' if 'scrounger' in c['id'] else 'watcher_survival_gap'
    emit('| '+c['id']+' | '+mean_metric(rows,key,True)+' | '+mean_metric(rows,key)+' |')
emit();emit('### Guard-harvest probes');emit()
emit('These are separate sensitivity cells. The probe allows current-cell harvest only after a paid guard turn whose own recovery took no food. It is not ordinary guarding and is not substituted into the primary guard contrasts. Values below are actual endpoint episode per-run means; full probe trajectories and baseline contrasts remain in the declared table and machine analysis.');emit()
emit('| Condition | Guard executions | Probe harvest | Harvest/guard (complete seeds only) | Survival |')
emit('|---|---:|---:|---|---:|')
for c in conditions:
    if 'guard-harvest' not in c['id']:continue
    rows=by[c['id']];emit('| '+c['id']+' | '+' | '.join(mean_metric(rows,k) for k in ['guard_executed','guard_probe_harvest','guard_probe_harvest_per_guard','survival'])+' |')
emit();emit('### Raw timing seconds (all separate timing seeds)');emit()
emit('All comparison workers joined before the serial timing phase. Raw full-run seconds include construction/breeding/bookkeeping and exclude sampling, replay and I/O; raw whole-tick seconds sum only replayed `World::step` calls. Returned generation records, actual elapsed ticks, live-at-tick-start agent-ticks including death ticks, terminals and survival are retained in the main timing table. No timing run or terminal is dropped.');emit()
emit('| Timing condition | Seed | Raw whole-tick seconds | Raw full-run seconds |')
emit('|---|---:|---:|---:|')
for t in a['timings']:
    emit('| '+t['condition_id']+' | '+str(t['seed'])+' | '+fmt(t['timing']['tick_seconds'])+' | '+fmt(t['timing']['runner_seconds'])+' |')
emit();emit('## Exact seedwise declared differences');emit()
emit('All 129 declarations are preserved below in manifest order. Each entry is `run_seed:A−B`; `absent` means undefined or unused, with the entire mean/interval/sign summary unavailable. Signs and descriptive 95% Student t intervals are in the declared table above. No generation, founder or duplicated panel cell is treated as an independent sample.');emit()
for contrast in measured['contrasts']:
    emit('<details>');emit('<summary>'+contrast['declaration']['id']+'</summary>');emit();emit('```text')
    values=contrast['seed_differences']
    for begin in range(1,41,10):emit('  '.join(str(seed)+':'+('absent' if values[str(seed)] is None else format(values[str(seed)],'.17g')) for seed in range(begin,begin+10)))
    emit('```');emit();emit('</details>');emit()
print('\n'.join(out))
```

</details>
